Let's detail full soft mirror optimizer:

   maxC = C.max()

   Wf = np.fft.fft(W, Sg)

   diag = W[2*npos] (where 2i < L)
   grad = q + p * diag

Function `_gradient(p, C, W, n, S, phase)`:
```
X = np.fft.fft(p, S) # We already have X
Y = phase * np.conjugate(X)
Wf = np.fft.fft(W, S)
H = np.fft.ifft(Y * Wf).real
q = H[n-1:n-1+n]
diag = W[2*np.arange(n)]
grad = q + p*diag
return grad
```

        g = grad - np.min(grad)
        scale = g.max() - g.min()

            ...
            continue

        # Try alphas: alpha, 0.5alpha, alpha*0.25
        accepted=False

           v_trial -= v_trial.max()
           v_trial = np.clip(v_trial, -B, 0)
           p_trial = np.exp(v_trial); p_trial /= p_trial.sum()
           m_trial = _maxC_fast(p_trial,n,score_size)
           delta = m_trial - M  # not score but Cmax diff
           if m_trial < M - 1e-16:
               accept
               alpha=min(alpha*1.05, 1.5)
               break
           if accept_worse and delta < T_C:  # T_C small
               accept
               break
        if not accepted:

Our formula for variable p_i only considered k-i=j; but p_i can appear as first factor (i variable) and as second factor (when i=k-i? no, second factor index k-i); derivative includes p_{k-i} if p_i appears as first factor in term i, and if p_i appears as second factor in term i' where i'=k-i (i.e., k constant, i'=j). Thus for a given p_i, in C_k there are two contributions if both i and k-i are in range: one from term i (first factor) giving p_{k-i}, and one from term k-i (second factor) giving p_i. But our formula q_i sum_j p_j W_{i+j} includes only first factor? Let's derive properly.

C_k = ∑_{i} p_i p_{k-i}, where k-i within [0,n-1]. Let S_k = {i: 0≤i<n, 0≤k-i<n}. Derivative wrt p_r:
- If r ∈ S_k, term i=r: derivative p_{k-r}.
- If k-r ∈ S_k (same condition), term i=k-r: derivative p_r.
If r = k-r (2r=k), these are the same term, derivative 2p_r.
Thus derivative = p_{k-r} + p_r if r and k-r distinct, else 2p_r. In general derivative = p_{k-r} + p_r for k≠2r, and 2p_r (which equals p_r+p_r) if 2r=k. So derivative = p_{k-r} + p_r for all? def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

`_active_weights(C, M, frac)`:
```
def _active_weights(C, M, frac, max_exp=50.0):
    L = len(C)
    scale = max(1e-300, M*frac)
    x = (M - C) / scale
    # x>=0, but due roundoff C may slightly >M, x<0. Use max(0)
    x = np.maximum(0.0, x)
    x[x > max_exp] = 0.0 # set weight 0? Actually exp(-large)=0. For x <= max_exp, exp; else 0.
    w = np.exp(-x)
    s = w.sum(dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       # fallback: top 16
       w=np.zeros(L)
       # choose topK
       top = np.argpartition(C, -16)[-16:] if L>16 else np.arange(L)
       w[top]=1.0
       s=w.sum()
    return w / s
```
But setting x>max_exp to 0 means w=0? We can do `w = np.where(x < max_exp, np.exp(-x), 0.0)`. If many coefficients all 0 due frac tiny, fallback top 16. If M=0, uniform.

Frac choice: Values: if frac=0.01, coefficients within 1% of M. If C differs by 0.001*M, weight 0.0003. Good. If all coefficients far (max unique), only max. Good.
Could power sharpen: w = w**p. Not necessary.

But if many coefficients have tiny negative due roundoff? M max, x=0. Good.

`_make_p_from_v(v, B)`:
```
def _v_to_p(v, B):
   v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
   vmax = v.max()
   if not finite: v=np.zeros(len(v))
   v = v - vmax
   v = np.clip(v, -B, 0.0)
   p = np.exp(v)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<1e-300: p=np.ones(len(v))/len(v)
   else: p/=s
   return p, v
```
But if v values all -inf? not.

`_p_to_v(p, B)`:

# now min >= -B
v -= v.max()
v = np.clip(v, -B, 0.0)

Line search apply:
```
def _apply_step(v, u, a, B):
    vt = v - a*u
    vmax = vt.max()
    if not np.isfinite(vmax):
       vt = np.zeros_like(v)
    else:
       vt = vt - vmax
    vt = np.clip(vt, -B, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(vt)
    s = p.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       p = np.ones_like(vt)/len(vt)
    else:
       p /= s
    return p, vt
```
Note: v input has max0; u in [0,1]; vt max maybe 0 if u min=0. Good.

def _base_starts(p, n, rng):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(p)
    # smoothed: convolve with kernel length min(21,n//10)
    k= max(1, min(31, n//200))
    kernel=np.ones(k)/k
    ps=np.convolve(p, kernel, mode='same')
    starts.append(np.maximum(ps,0))
    # high-frequency? p_abs? no
    # one-sided binomial
    b=_binomial_start(n)
    starts.append(b)
    # binomial with right tail 0.1
    for r in (0.05, 0.2):
        starts.append(b + r*b[::-1])
    # p with log noise 1%
    _, v = _p_to_v(p, 70)
    pnoise = _v_to_p(v + 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n),70)[0]
    starts.append(pnoise)
    # U edge: b + b[::-1]
    starts.append(b + b[::-1])
    # Maybe p edge-enhanced: p * (1 + 0.2*(edges))? not.
    # cleaned & normalize in scoring
```

Let's code `_binomial_start(n)`:
```
def _binomial_start(n):
    if n <=1:
       return np.ones(n) if n>0 else np.ones(1)
    a=np.ones(n)
    prev=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       prev *= (2*i -1)/(2*i)
       a[i]=prev
    s=a.sum()
    if s>0: a/=s
    else: a=np.ones(n)/n
    return a
```
For n=6121 loop fine.

if p.size != n: p=p[:n] or pad? We only pass correct.
p=np.maximum(p,0)
s=p.sum()
if not finite or s<1e-12:
   p=np.ones(n); s=n
p/=s
return p
```

Yes, `irfft(F*F, n)` expects the real spectrum (first half) of product. Since product of two real-signal spectra of same sequence has Hermitian symmetry; the rfft half is F*F (not F*conj(F)). For convolution a*a, yes. Numpy's irfft assumes conjugate symmetry; if we supply F*F, it effectively constructs full spectrum with negative freqs as conj of positive. Does that equal full FFT product? For real FFT, negative frequencies are conjugates of positive; for product X*X, negative should be X[-k]^2. Is that equal to conj(X[k]^2)? For real X, X[-k]=conj(X[k]], so X[-k]^2 = conj(X[k]^2). Yes. So F*F is correct. Good.

def _optimize_sequence(p_init, deadline, opts, rng, n, Sscore, Sgrad, phase, name):
    B = opts.get('B', 70.0)
    p = _normalize_p(p_init, n)
    v = _p_to_v(p, B)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_C = _score_fast(p, n, Sscore)
    alpha = opts.get('alpha', 0.5)
    alpha_max = opts.get('alpha_max', 2.0)
    noise_sigma = opts.get('noise', 0.03)
    stuck_s = opts.get('stuck', 60.0)
    frac_start=...
    frac_end=...
    frac_tau = max(20.0, opts.get('tau', 0.4)*(deadline - time.monotonic()))
    L = 2*n - 1
    tol = max(1e-18, 1e-12 * best_C)
    last_imp = time.monotonic()
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    reject_count=0
    it=0
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    phase_dur = max(1.0, deadline - start_t)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
       now=time.monotonic()
       elapsed=now-start_t
       frac_sched = frac_end + (frac_start - frac_end) * math.exp(-elapsed / frac_tau)
       frac = max(frac_sched, 0.0008)
       # stuck
       if now - last_imp > stuck_s:
          ratio = min(3.0, (now-last_imp)/stuck_s)
          sigma = noise_sigma * (1.0 + ratio)
          v_no = v + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
          v_no = v_no - v_no.max()
          v_no = np.clip(v_no, -B, 0.0)
          p = np.exp(v_no); s=p.sum()
          if s<...: uniform
          p /= s
          v = v_no
          last_imp=now
          frac = max(frac, 0.05)
          continue
       F = np.fft.fft(p, Sgrad)
       C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
       C = np.where(C < 0.0, 0.0, C)
       M = float(C.max())
       if M < best_C - tol:
          best_C = M; best_p=p.copy(); last_imp=now
          # add pool? local only
       W = _active_weights(C, M, frac)
       Wf = np.fft.fft(W, Sgrad)
       H = np.fft.ifft(phase * np.conjugate(F) * Wf).real
       q = H[n-1:n-1+n]
       q = np.where(q < 0.0, 0.0, q)
       qmax = float(q.max())
       if qmax <= 1e-300:
          # no direction; small noise
          v_no = v + 0.005*rng.standard_normal(n); ...
          last_imp=now; it+=1; continue
       qmin = float(q.min())
       scale = qmax - qmin
       if scale <= 1e-300:
          # q constant but C not all active? maybe active W not with peaks? broaden frac
          frac = min(0.5, frac*5)
          continue
       u = (q - qmin) / scale
       # line search
       accepted=False
       a = alpha
       # Try main
       vt = v - a*u; vtv=vt.max(); vt=vt-vtv; vt=np.clip(vt,-B,0)
       p_new=np.exp(vt); s=p_new.sum(); if s...; p_new/=s
       m_new = _score_fast(p_new,n,Sscore)
       if m_new < M - tol:
           accepted=True; p=p_new; v=vt; alpha=min(alpha*1.05, alpha_max); reject_count=0
           if m_new < best_C - tol: best_C=m_new; best_p=p.copy(); last_imp=now
           # else if not improve best but improve current, update current last_imp? yes if improves current. We can set last_imp when current M decreases even if not best:
           # Actually if m_new < M but not best? best maybe smaller. last_imp global best; but for stuck, want improve current. Define current improve? Use `cur_best`? We only track best. We'll update last_imp if m_new < M (current improves), not just best.
       else:
           # try half if alpha>0.08
           if alpha > 0.1:
              a2=0.5*alpha; ...
              if m2 < M - tol: accept
           if not accepted:
              alpha = max(alpha*0.75, 0.02)
              reject_count +=1
              # maybe accept tiny worsen if stuck? optional
       # current improvement tracking: If accepted and m_tri < M (already) set last_imp_current? Let's have last_imp_best and last_imp_cur.
```

Let's implement `_active_weights` with softmax rather than threshold. Code:
```
def _active_weights(C, M, frac):
    L=len(C)
    if M <= 0: w=np.ones(L); return w/L
    scale=max(1e-300, M*frac)
    x=(M-C)/scale
    x=np.where(x<0,0.0,x)
    # zero out too small
    sel=x < 60.0
    w=np.zeros(L)
    w[sel]=np.exp(-x[sel])
    s=w.sum(dtype=np.float64)
    if s<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w=np.zeros(L)
        top = min(32, L)
        idx = np.argpartition(C, -top)[-top:] if L>top else np.arange(L)
        w[idx]=1.0
        s=w.sum()
    return w/s
```
If frac=0.0008, scale small, x for second peak maybe >60 -> only max. Good.

But if C contains values slightly above M due roundoff? M is max so none. If M very small, scale maybe 0; fallback.

Now, about `np.argpartition(C, -top)` with top = L maybe; if L > top. `idx = np.argpartition(C, L-top)[-top:]` if top. Use `np.argsort(C)[-top:]` for small top simpler (L=12241, sort expensive but fallback rare). Use `np.argpartition` correctly:
```
if L > top:
   idx = np.argpartition(C, L - top)[-top:]
else:
   idx = np.arange(L)
```
Works if L-top >=0. If top=1, L-top=L-1 <L. Good. If L=1, else.

Now, start blend creation:
```
def _make_starts(p0,n,rng):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(p0.copy())
    if n > 10:
       k = max(1, min(21, n//100))
       ker=np.ones(k)/k
       starts.append(np.convolve(p0, ker, mode='same'))
    b = _binomial_start(n)
    starts.append(b)
    starts.append(b[::-1]) # same as b? not same, score same but different orientation? Conv score same due reversal? The score of reversed is same, but p shape reversed. If f_init not symmetric? score same. It might provide a different basin? Since objective reversal invariant, reversed of optimum same score. But if f_init asymmetrical, reversed is mirror, same. Include anyway.
    starts.append(b + b[::-1])
    starts.append(0.9*b + 0.1*b[::-1])
    un = np.ones(n)/n
    starts.append(0.9*p0 + 0.1*un)
    starts.append(0.7*p0 + 0.3*b)
    starts.append(0.85*p0 + 0.15*b[::-1])
    # tiny noise
    _, v = _p_to_v(p0,70)
    pnoise = _v_to_p(v + 0.015*rng.standard_normal(n),70)[0]
    starts.append(pnoise)
    # high edge mixture: 0.5*p0 +0.5*edge (b+rev)
    starts.append(0.5*p0 + 0.5*(b+b[::-1]))

def _apply_and_score(v, u, a, B, n, Sscore):
    vt=v - a*u

    p=np.exp(vt)

    if not finite or s<...: p=np.ones(n)/n
    else: p/=s
    m=_score_fast(p,n,Sscore)
    return p, vt, m
```

Resampling:
```
def _resample probability p0,n0,n1:
    if n1==n0: return p0.copy()
    if n0 ==1: return ones(n1)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n1)
    p1 = np.interp(x1, x0, p0)
    p1 = np.maximum(p1,0); p1/=p1.sum()
    return p1
```
This may smooth edges. Could also multiply by (n0/n1)? Normalization handles.

Now, let's think about a possible mathematical shortcut: Since q gradient is linear in p, could use a Newton-like solve to equalize q more quickly. We can implement a fixed-point balancing step occasionally:
Given current W and q, c = float(np.dot(p, q))? If W sum1, c = avg active C (not necessarily M). We want p' with A p'=c 1. The multiplicative update p' ∝ p * (c/q)^β. We can try β=1 (line search) alongside mirror. This might converge faster to KKT. Let's add a balancing candidate in line search when main mirror fails or every 20 iterations.

Function to generate balancing direction:
```
def _balancing_direction(q, B):
   qmax=q.max(); qmin_pos = qmax*1e-12
   qs=np.maximum(q, qmin_pos)
   d = np.log(qmax/qs)  # zero at qmax, positive for small q
   # adding constant irrelevant; umax = d - d.min(); scale = umax.max(); u=umax/scale
```
This increases coordinates with small q (ratio >1) and decreases high q. Compared to mirror u=(q-qmin)/scale which decreases high q but leaves min unchanged. Balancing more aggressive for zero q. Use `alpha_bal` maybe 0.5*alpha. We can in line search try main mirror, if fails try balancing, then half mirror.

`_normalize_p` handles list length not n? We pass n. If p array shorter/longer, we can resize:

For n from global, starts correct. Use robust.

```
def _normalize_p(p, n):
    arr=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n); tmp[:arr.size]=arr; arr=tmp
    elif arr.size > n:
       arr=arr[:n]
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
       arr=np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
       s=float(n)
    arr/=s
    return arr
```
If n=0? not possible. If n from empty global, we set n=6121. In propose, if heights invalid, n=6121.

`_p_to_v`:
```
def _p_to_v(p, B):
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=p.sum()
   if not finite or s<1e-12: p=np.ones_like(p); s=float(p.size)
   p=p/s
   pmax=float(p.max())
   if pmax <=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/p.size; pmax=1/p.size
   floor=pmax*math.exp(-B)
   p=np.maximum(p, floor)
   s=p.sum(); p/=s
   pmax=float(p.max())
   v=np.log(p/pmax)
   v -= v.max()
   v=np.clip(v, -B, 0.0)
   return p, v
```
`math.exp(-B)` with B=70 constant; can precompute but fine.

def _v_to_p(v, B):
  vmax=v.max(); if not finite: v=zeros; else v=v-vmax
  v=np.clip(v,-B,0)
  p=np.exp(v); s=p.sum(); if s<... uniform; else p/=s
  return p
```
Use in `_make_starts` and stuck perturb? In optimizer, stuck perturb duplicated for speed; can call `_v_to_p` but need v assignment. It returns p only; we can recompute v? But we set v from noise then p by exp. Use inline for speed.

`_score_fast` robust:
```
def _score_fast(p, n, Sscore):
    p=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != n: p=_normalize_p(p,n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return np.inf
    if p.sum() < 1e-12: p=np.ones(n)/n
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, Sscore)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F, Sscore)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0: m=0.0
    return m
```
Calling `np.all(np.isfinite)` each score adds n overhead; not too bad. In loop p known finite; we can skip in `_score_fast` for speed? But candidates finite. Use a faster version for internal and safe? We can have `_score_fast_nocheck`. To keep simple, in loop p finite, but _apply_and_score uses _score_fast; p finite. Checking finite 6121 ~0.05ms, okay. Could omit for speed. Let's omit internal but handle exceptions? We'll trust p. At start scoring, p normalized finite. For final, use official. Use:
```
def _score_fast(p,n,Sscore):
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,Sscore)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,Sscore)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(C.max())
    return m if finite and m>=0 else 0.0
```
If p contains NaN, FFT returns NaN; return inf? `if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf`. Good.

Now, `_optimize_sequence` code with details.

Let's write it carefully:
```
def _optimize_sequence(p_init, n, Sscore, Sgrad, phase, deadline, opts, rng):
    B = float(opts.get('B', 70.0))
    p = _normalize_p(p_init, n)
    p, v = _p_to_v(p, B)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_C = _score_fast(p, n, Sscore)
    L = 2*n - 1
    name = str(opts.get('name', 'opt'))
    alpha = float(opts.get('alpha', 0.5))
    alpha_max = float(opts.get('alpha_max', 2.0))
    noise_sigma = float(opts.get('noise', 0.03))
    stuck_s = float(opts.get('stuck', 60.0))
    frac_start = float(opts.get('frac_start', 0.08))
    frac_end = float(opts.get('frac_end', 0.004))
    tau_frac = float(opts.get('tau', 0.5))
    use_balance = bool(opts.get('balance', True))
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    phase_dur = max(1.0, deadline - start_t)
    frac_tau = max(20.0, tau_frac * phase_dur)
    tol = max(1e-18, 1e-12 * max(1e-6, best_C))
    last_imp = start_t
    last_print = 0.0
    reject_count = 0
    it = 0
    frac_boost = 0.0
    Bmin = -B
    print(name + ": start score=%.6f C=%.10f B=%.1f" % (2.0*n*best_C, best_C, B))
    while True:
       now = time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline - 0.5:
          break
       elapsed = now - start_t
       frac_sched = frac_end + (frac_start - frac_end) * math.exp(-elapsed / frac_tau)
       frac = max(frac_sched, frac_boost, 0.0008)
       # stuck
       if now - last_imp > stuck_s:
           ratio = min(3.0, (now - last_imp) / max(1e-6, stuck_s))
           sigma = noise_sigma * (1.0 + ratio)
           v_no = v + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
           vmax = float(v_no.max()) if np.isfinite(v_no.max()) else 0.0
           v_no = v_no - vmax
           v_no = np.clip(v_no, Bmin, 0.0)
           p_new = np.exp(v_no)
           s = float(p_new.sum())
           if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
               p_new = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
               v_no = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
           else:
               p_new /= s
           p = p_new
           v = v_no
           frac_boost = max(frac_boost, 0.12)
           last_imp = now
           continue
       # compute C and q
       F = np.fft.fft(p, Sgrad)
       C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
       C = np.where(C < 0.0, 0.0, C)
       M = float(C.max())
       if not np.isfinite(M):
           # random restart small
           p = rng.standard_normal(n); p=np.abs(p)+0.1; p/=p.sum(); p,v=_p_to_v(p,B); M=best_C; continue
       if M < best_C - tol:
           best_C = M; best_p = p.copy(); last_imp = now
       W = _active_weights(C, M, frac)
       Wf = np.fft.fft(W, Sgrad)
       H = np.fft.ifft(phase * np.conjugate(F) * Wf).real
       q = H[n-1:n-1+n]
       q = np.where(q < 0.0, 0.0, q)
       qmax = float(q.max())
       if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 1e-300:
           frac_boost = max(frac_boost, min(0.5, frac_boost*2.0 + 0.03))
           reject_count += 1
           it +=1
           if now - last_print > 15.0:
              print(...); last_print=now
           continue
       qmin = float(q.min())
       scale = qmax - qmin
       if scale <= 1e-300 * qmax:
           frac_boost = max(frac_boost, min(0.5, frac_boost*2.0 + 0.03))
           # q constant
           reject_count += 1
           it +=1
           if now - last_print >15: print; continue
       u = (q - qmin) / scale
       # balancing direction optional
       u_bal = None
       if use_balance and (qmin < 0.25 * qmax) and (reject_count > 0 or it % 8 == 0):
           qsafe = np.maximum(q, qmax * 1e-12)
           d = -np.log(qsafe / qmax)  # >=0
           dmin = float(d.min())
           drange = float(d.max()) - dmin
           if np.isfinite(drange) and drange > 1e-30:
               u_bal = (d - dmin) / drange
           else:
               u_bal = None
       directions = []
       directions.append((u, alpha))
       if u_bal is not None:
           directions.append((u_bal, min(0.8, alpha)))
       directions.append((u, 0.5*alpha))
       accepted=False
       trial_M = M
       a_accepted=0.0
       for dir_u, a_trial in directions:
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(dir_u)):
               continue
           p_try, v_try, m_try = _apply_and_score(v, dir_u, a_trial, B, n, Sscore)
           if m_try < trial_M - tol:  # trial_M old M; if first failed, still old M (p unchanged)
               p = p_try; v = v_try; accepted=True; a_accepted=a_trial; trial_M=m_try; break
           # If we accepted? no.
       if accepted:
           # update best if m_try < best_C? trial_M is new m
           if trial_M < best_C - tol:
               best_C = trial_M; best_p = p.copy()
           last_imp = now
           reject_count = 0
           frac_boost = max(0.0, frac_boost * 0.3)
           # if u direction? alpha update based on direction? We don't know if balance; but can increase alpha if small
           alpha = min(alpha * 1.05, alpha_max)
       else:
           alpha = max(alpha * 0.75, 0.02)
           reject_count += 1
           if reject_count > 120:
               # mild perturbation
               sigma = max(0.005, noise_sigma * 0.2)
               v_no = v + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
               vmax = float(v_no.max()) if np.isfinite(v_no.max()) else 0.0
               v_no = v_no - vmax
               v_no = np.clip(v_no, Bmin, 0.0)
               p = np.exp(v_no); s=float(p.sum()); if...; p/=s; v=v_no
               reject_count=0
               frac_boost=max(frac_boost, 0.05)
       it += 1
       if now - last_print > 15.0 and it % 50 == 0:
           print(name + f" it={it} best_score={2.0*n*best_C:.6f} cur_C={M:.10f} frac={frac:.4f} alpha={alpha:.3f} rej={reject_count}")
           last_print = now
    # final update if current p better
    cur_M = _score_fast(p, n, Sscore)
    if cur_M < best_C:
        best_C = cur_M; best_p = p.copy()
    print(name + " finished score=%.6f" % (2.0*n*best_C))
    return best_p, best_C
```

Be careful in line search directions: If first direction fails and we evaluate second, p still old, trial_M should remain M, not updated. We set `trial_M = M`; in loop if fail, don't change. for dir_u,a_trial in directions:
   p_try,v_try,m_try = _apply_and_score(...)
   if m_try < M - tol:
      p=p_try; v=v_try; accepted=True; m_acc=m_try; break
```
Then if accepted use m_acc. Good.

Now, proof of gradient one more time. Use previous code's `_topk_grad`: For active idx, loop lo=max(0, idx-n+1), hi=min(n, idx+1), grad[lo:hi] += 2.0 * f[idx - np.arange(lo, hi)]. Now, let's think about if p has sum exactly 1 but v not exactly log p due clipping; using v for step and p for gradient. The p is normalized from v but v may not correspond exactly (constant + clip? But v is relative shape of p before normalization; because p∝exp(v), constant difference okay). Good.

Now, possible issue in `_apply_and_score`: It generates `p_try = np.exp(vt)`, where vt max maybe 0 but if all vt = -B and max 0 after subtract? Actually if v all -B? We maintain max 0, min -B. If u all zeros, vt=v, max 0. If u has min where qmin, vt for those coords may be unchanged. If v has max0 at many coords, okay. p sum at least number of max coords *1 >0. Good.

Now, output official score maybe printed with many decimals. Good.

Let's review final code for no lambda / closures. We'll use `operator.itemgetter`; no lambda. Use f-strings no lambda. Use `np.where`. Good.

Now, write code in final. Ensure imports at top: `import numpy as np`, `import time`, `import math`, `import operator`.

Potential issue: `np.fft.fft` with real inputs returns complex; using `np.conjugate(F)` inside `H` every iteration creates new array. Phase multiply and conj. `phase * np.conjugate(F) * Wf` creates two temp arrays. Could optimize: `Y = phase * np.conjugate(F); H = np.fft.ifft(Y * Wf).real`. Fine.

Memory of pool: If we add p copies, each 6121*8 =49KB; 10 copies 0.5MB. Fine.

Now, one possible flaw: In line search, we evaluate score for p_try using `_score_fast`, which computes maxC from p_try. If p_try improves old max but changes new max at a different coefficient, score captures. Good.

Now, if q scale tiny, we broaden frac but don't update last_imp, so stuck triggers eventually. Good.

Now, if no direction but q constant and frac_boost reaches 0.5, next loop W broad; if q still constant (e.g., uniform), reject_count increases. Stuck will perturb after stuck_s. Good.

Now, if broad phase from start2 with very noisy p, objective may be high, gradient direction moves. Good.

```python
"""
Optimizer for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum a)^2.

The algorithm normalizes coefficients to the simplex and minimizes the
auto-convolution peak.  It uses FFT-based exact auto-convolution, a
soft-active-set subgradient computed from near-peak lags, mirror-descent
updates in log-weights with adaptive step size, line searching in the
exact objective, simulated-restart perturbation when stuck, and several
phases with different active-set widths.  A small pool of good candidates
is checked with the official evaluator before returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
import operator


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _make_phase(n, size):
    k = np.arange(size, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.exp(-2j * np.pi * (n - 1) * k / float(size))


def _normalize_p(p, n):
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:arr.size] = arr
        arr = tmp
    elif arr.size > n:
        arr = arr[:n]
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    arr /= s
    return arr


def _p_to_v(p, b_bound):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.size)
    p /= s

    pmax = float(p.max())
    if pmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(pmax):
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
        pmax = 1.0 / p.size

    floor = pmax * math.exp(-b_bound)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
    else:
        p /= s

    pmax = float(p.max())
    if pmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(pmax):
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
        pmax = 1.0 / p.size

    v = np.log(p / pmax)
    v = v - float(v.max())
    v = np.clip(v, -b_bound, 0.0)
    return p, v


def _v_to_p(v, b_bound):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    vmax = float(v.max())
    if not np.isfinite(vmax):
        v = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        v = v - vmax
    v = np.clip(v, -b_bound, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(v)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / v.size
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _score_fast(p, n, size):
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return m


def _active_weights(c, m, frac):
    lam = c.size
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(lam, dtype=np.float64) / lam

    scale = max(1e-300, m * float(frac))
    x = (m - c) / scale
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 60.0)
    x = np.where(x < 0.0, 0.0, x)

    keep = x < 60.0
    w = np.zeros(lam, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.any(keep):
        w[keep] = np.exp(-x[keep])
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        top = min(32, lam)
        if lam > top:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, lam - top)[-(top):]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(lam)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        s = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(lam, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(lam)
    return w / s


def _apply_and_score(v, u, alpha, b_bound, n, score_size):
    vt = v - float(alpha) * u
    vmax = float(vt.max())
    if not np.isfinite(vmax):
        vt = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        vt = vt - vmax
    vt = np.clip(vt, -b_bound, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(vt)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        vt = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p, vt, _score_fast(p, n, score_size)


def _binomial_start(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cur = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        cur *= float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
        a[i] = cur
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def _make_starts(p0, n, rng):
    starts = []
    starts.append(p0.copy())

    if n > 12:
        k = max(1, min(21, n // 100))
        ker = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
        starts.append(np.convolve(p0, ker, mode="same"))

    b = _binomial_start(n)
    starts.append(b)
    starts.append(b[::-1])
    starts.append(b + b[::-1])
    starts.append(0.90 * b + 0.10 * b[::-1])
    starts.append(0.70 * p0 + 0.30 * b)
    starts.append(0.85 * p0 + 0.15 * b[::-1])
    starts.append(0.90 * p0 + 0.10 * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n))
    starts.append(0.50 * p0 + 0.50 * (b + b[::-1]))

    _, v0 = _p_to_v(p0, 70.0)
    pnoise = _v_to_p(v0 + 0.015 * rng.standard_normal(n), 70.0)
    starts.append(pnoise)

    return starts


def _optimize_sequence(p_init, n, score_size, grad_size, phase, deadline, opts, rng):
    b_bound = float(opts.get("b_bound", 70.0))
    name = str(opts.get("name", "opt"))
    alpha0 = float(opts.get("alpha", 0.5))
    alpha_max = float(opts.get("alpha_max", 2.0))
    noise0 = float(opts.get("noise", 0.03))
    stuck_s = float(opts.get("stuck", 60.0))
    frac_start = float(opts.get("frac_start", 0.08))
    frac_end = float(opts.get("frac_end", 0.004))
    tau = float(opts.get("tau", 0.5))
    use_balance = bool(opts.get("balance", True))

    p = _normalize_p(p_init, n)
    p, v = _p_to_v(p, b_bound)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = _score_fast(p, n, score_size)

    lam = 2 * n - 1
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    phase_dur = max(1.0, deadline - start_t)
    frac_tau = max(20.0, tau * phase_dur)
    seq_tol = max(1e-18, 1e-12 * max(1e-6, best_c))

    alpha = alpha0
    reject_count = 0
    frac_boost = 0.0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    last_imp = start_t

    print(
        name +
        ": start score=%.6f c=%.10f frac=[%.4f -> %.5f] alpha=%.3f"
        % (2.0 * n * best_c, best_c, frac_start, frac_end, alpha)
    )

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 0.6:
            break

        elapsed = max(1e-9, now - start_t)
        frac_sched = frac_end + (frac_start - frac_end) * math.exp(-elapsed / frac_tau)
        frac = max(frac_sched, frac_boost, 0.0008)

        if now - last_imp > stuck_s:
            ratio = min(3.0, (now - last_imp) / max(1e-9, stuck_s))
            sigma = noise0 * (1.0 + ratio)
            vn = v + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
            vmax = float(vn.max())
            if not np.isfinite(vmax):
                vn = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                vn = vn - vmax
            vn = np.clip(vn, -b_bound, 0.0)
            pn = np.exp(vn)
            s = float(pn.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
                pn = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
                vn = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                pn /= s
            p = pn
            v = vn
            frac_boost = max(frac_boost, 0.12)
            alpha = max(alpha, 0.5)
            last_imp = now
            it += 1
            continue

        F = np.fft.fft(p, grad_size)
        C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:lam]
        C = np.where(C < 0.0, 0.0, C)
        cur_c = float(C.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cur_c):
            p = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.1
            p /= p.sum()
            p, v = _p_to_v(p, b_bound)
            it += 1
            continue

        if cur_c < best_c - seq_tol:
            best_c = cur_c
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_imp = now

        W = _active_weights(C, cur_c, frac)
        WF = np.fft.fft(W, grad_size)
        H = np.fft.ifft(phase * np.conjugate(F) * WF).real
        q = H[n - 1 : n - 1 + n]
        q = np.where(q < 0.0, 0.0, q)

        qmax = float(q.max())
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 1e-300:
            frac_boost = max(frac_boost, min(0.5, frac_boost * 2.0 + 0.03))
            reject_count += 1
            it += 1
            if now - last_print > 15.0:
                print(name + ": stalled q=0, broadening")
                last_print = now
            continue

        qmin = float(q.min())
        sc = qmax - qmin
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 1e-300 * qmax:
            frac_boost = max(frac_boost, min(0.5, frac_boost * 2.0 + 0.03))
            reject_count += 1
            it += 1
            if now - last_print > 15.0:
                print(name + ": q almost constant, broadening")
                last_print = now
            continue

        u = (q - qmin) / sc

        ub = None
        if use_balance and (qmin < 0.25 * qmax) and (reject_count > 0 or (it % 8) == 0):
            qsafe = np.maximum(q, qmax * 1e-12)
            d = -np.log(qsafe / qmax)
            dmin = float(d.min())
            drng = float(d.max()) - dmin
            if np.isfinite(drng) and drng > 1e-30:
                ub = (d - dmin) / drng
            else:
                ub = None

        directions = []
        directions.append((u, alpha))
        if ub is not None:
            directions.append((ub, min(0.8, alpha)))
        directions.append((u, 0.5 * alpha))

        accepted = False
        new_c = cur_c
        for dir_u, a_trial in directions:
            if dir_u is None:
                continue
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(dir_u)):
                continue
            pn, vn, mn = _apply_and_score(v, dir_u, a_trial, b_bound, n, score_size)
            if mn < cur_c - seq_tol:
                p = pn
                v = vn
                new_c = mn
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if new_c < best_c - seq_tol:
                best_c = new_c
                best_p = p.copy()
            last_imp = now
            reject_count = 0
            frac_boost = max(0.0, frac_boost * 0.3)
            alpha = min(alpha * 1.05, alpha_max)
        else:
            alpha = max(alpha * 0.75, 0.02)
            reject_count += 1
            if reject_count > 120:
                sigma = max(0.005, noise0 * 0.2)
                vn = v + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)
                vmax = float(vn.max())
                if not np.isfinite(vmax):
                    vn = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                else:
                    vn = vn - vmax
                vn = np.clip(vn, -b_bound, 0.0)
                pn = np.exp(vn)
                s = float(pn.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
                    pn = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
                    vn = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                else:
                    pn /= s
                p = pn
                v = vn
                reject_count = 0
                frac_boost = max(frac_boost, 0.05)

        it += 1
        if now - last_print > 15.0 and (it % 40 == 0 or accepted):
            print(
                name +
                " it=%d best_score=%.6f cur_c=%.10f frac=%.5f alpha=%.3f rej=%d"
                % (it, 2.0 * n * best_c, cur_c, frac, alpha, reject_count)
            )
            last_print = now

    final_c = _score_fast(p, n, score_size)
    if final_c < best_c:
        best_c = final_c
        best_p = p.copy()

    print(name + ": finished score=%.6f" % (2.0 * n * best_c))
    return best_p, best_c


def _optimize_for_p(p_init, n, deadline, opts, rng):
    score_size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    grad_size = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    phase = _make_phase(n, grad_size)
    return _optimize_sequence(p_init, n, score_size, grad_size, phase, deadline, opts, rng)


def propose_candidate(seed=1337, budget_s=2370, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2370.0
    budget = min(budget, 2380.0)
    budget = max(budget, 120.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    heights = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if heights is None:
        n = 6121
        p0 = _binomial_start(n)
    else:
        h = np.asarray(heights, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if h.size == 0:
            n = 6121
            p0 = _binomial_start(n)
        else:
            n = int(h.size)
            p0 = _normalize_p(h, n)

    if n <= 0:
        n = 6121
        p0 = _binomial_start(n)

    print("Length n =", n)
    score_size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    starts = _make_starts(p0, n, rng)
    scored = []
    for s in starts:
        pp = _normalize_p(s, n)
        cc = _score_fast(pp, n, score_size)
        if np.isfinite(cc):
            scored.append((cc, pp))
    if not scored:
        pp = _binomial_start(n)
        cc = _score_fast(pp, n, score_size)
        scored.append((cc, pp))

    scored.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    best_c = scored[0][0]
    best_p = scored[0][1].copy()
    best_score = 2.0 * n * best_c

    pool = []
    for cc, pp in scored[:3]:
        pool.append((2.0 * n * cc, pp.copy()))
    pool.append((best_score, best_p.copy()))

    print("Best initial score =", best_score)
    print("Top initial scores =", [("%.6f" % (2.0 * n * cc)) for cc, _ in scored[:5]])

    remaining = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    opts1 = {
        "name": "phase1-refine",
        "alpha": 0.5,
        "alpha_max": 2.0,
        "noise": 0.03,
        "stuck": 60.0,
        "frac_start": 0.08,
        "frac_end": 0.004,
        "tau": 0.5,
        "b_bound": 70.0,
        "balance": True,
    }
    alloc1 = 0.50 * remaining
    d1 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc1)
    p1, c1 = _optimize_for_p(best_p, n, d1, opts1, rng)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_c = c1
        best_p = p1.copy()
        best_score = 2.0 * n * best_c
        pool.append((best_score, best_p.copy()))
        print("After phase1 score =", best_score)

    remaining = max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    broad_frac_time = 0.20 if best_score > 1.507 else 0.05
    alloc2 = broad_frac_time * remaining
    if alloc2 > 5.0:
        opts2 = {
            "name": "phase2-broad",
            "alpha": 0.7,
            "alpha_max": 2.0,
            "noise": 0.11,
            "stuck": 45.0,
            "frac_start": 0.28,
            "frac_end": 0.03,
            "tau": 0.4,
            "b_bound": 70.0,
            "balance": True,
        }
        if len(scored) > 1:
            start2 = scored[1][1].copy()
        else:
            start2 = best_p.copy()
        d2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc2)
        p2, c2 = _optimize_for_p(start2, n, d2, opts2, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_score = 2.0 * n * best_c
            pool.append((best_score, best_p.copy()))
            print("After phase2 score =", best_score)

    remaining = max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 30.0:
        opts3 = {
            "name": "phase3-mid",
            "alpha": 0.9,
            "alpha_max": 2.2,
            "noise": 0.018,
            "stuck": 50.0,
            "frac_start": 0.03,
            "frac_end": 0.002,
            "tau": 0.6,
            "b_bound": 70.0,
            "balance": True,
        }
        alloc3 = 0.50 * remaining
        d3 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc3)
        p3, c3 = _optimize_for_p(best_p, n, d3, opts3, rng)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_p = p3.copy()
            best_score = 2.0 * n * best_c
            pool.append((best_score, best_p.copy()))
            print("After phase3 score =", best_score)

    remaining = max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if remaining > 20.0:
        opts4 = {
            "name": "phase4-fine",
            "alpha": 1.2,
            "alpha_max": 2.5,
            "noise": 0.005,
            "stuck": 30.0,
            "frac_start": 0.008,
            "frac_end": 0.001,
            "tau": 0.7,
            "b_bound": 70.0,
            "balance": True,
        }
        d4 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(0.0, remaining - 8.0))
        p4, c4 = _optimize_for_p(best_p, n, d4, opts4, rng)
        if c4 < best_c:
            best_c = c4
            best_p = p4.copy()
            best_score = 2.0 * n * best_c
            pool.append((best_score, best_p.copy()))
            print("After phase4 score =", best_score)

    print("Pre-evaluator best score =", best_score)

    pool.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    max_evals = 3 if n <= 9000 else 1
    final_p = best_p.copy()
    final_score = best_score

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 5.0:
        for score_est, cand in pool[:max_evals]:
            cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            try:
                sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in cand])
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
                final_p = cand.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        fallback = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fallback])
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            final_score = sc
            final_p = fallback
        else:
            final_p = fallback
            final_score = 2.0 * n * _score_fast(final_p, n, score_size)

    final_p = np.asarray(final_p, dtype=np.float64)
    final_p = np.clip(final_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_p = np.where(np.isfinite(final_p), final_p, 0.0)
    s = float(final_p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        final_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)

    print("Final reported score =", final_score)
    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
